# Paper catalogue

Snapshot: **2026-10-23** · **1,701** confirmed records across **47** journals.

[Interactive website](https://awesome-medicalai-nature.grandowlet6.chatgpt.site/) · [Methodology](METHODOLOGY.md) · [JSON](data/catalogue.json)

This is the confirmed catalogue, not an exhaustive estimate of journal-wide biomedical AI publication shares. Every indexed publication has a paper-specific interpretation; source and field verification levels remain explicit.
